    <credit-words default-x="101" default-y="331" font-family="serif" font-size="13" font-weight="bold">note the D chord used in the 1st measure as a V chord of the next G chord. This smooths out the</credit-words>
  </credit>
  <credit page="1">
    <credit-words default-x="101" default-y="312" font-family="serif" font-size="13" font-weight="bold">transition to the G chord. This technique of putting in V chords right before another chord will be</credit-words>
  </credit>
  <credit page="1">
    <credit-words default-x="253" default-y="272" font-family="sans-serif" font-style="italic" font-size="13" font-weight="bold">9</credit-words>
  </credit>
  <credit page="1">
    <credit-words default-x="97" default-y="116" font-family="serif" font-size="14" font-weight="bold">of the notes in this type of style.</credit-words>
  </credit>
